# Identity and Access Management IAM roles and permissions

## Signature / Usage

Predefined roles of `iam` (role ID, description, included permissions). The permissions table below lists the roles that contain each permission.

### Roles

### Access Policy Admin (`roles/iam.accessPolicyAdmin`)

- Launch stage: Beta
- Access Policy admin role, with permissions to read and modify access policies, and to bind and unbind access policies to targets.
- Permissions (9): `iam.accesspolicies.*`, `iam.accesspolicies.bind`, `iam.accesspolicies.create`, `iam.accesspolicies.delete`, `iam.accesspolicies.get`, `iam.accesspolicies.list`, `iam.accesspolicies.searchPolicyBindings`, `iam.accesspolicies.unbind`, `iam.accesspolicies.update`

### IAM Admin (`roles/iam.admin`)

- Full administrative access to all IAM resources
- Permissions (164): `iam.accesspolicies.*`, `iam.accesspolicies.bind`, `iam.accesspolicies.create`, `iam.accesspolicies.delete`, `iam.accesspolicies.get`, `iam.accesspolicies.list`, ### Principal Access Boundary Policy Viewer (`roles/iam.principalAccessBoundaryViewer`)

- Read-only access to Principal Access Boundary policies and their associated bindings.
- Permissions (3): `iam.principalaccessboundarypolicies.get`, `iam.principalaccessboundarypolicies.list`, `iam.principalaccessboundarypolicies.searchPolicyBindings`

### Role Administrator (`roles/iam.roleAdmin`)

- Provides access to all custom roles in the project. This role can only be granted at the project level.
- Permissions (13): `iam.roles.*`, `iam.roles.create`, `iam.roles.createTagBinding`, `iam.roles.delete`, `iam.roles.deleteTagBinding`, `iam.roles.get`, `iam.roles.list`, `iam.roles.listEffectiveTags`, `iam.roles.listTagBindings`, `iam.roles.undelete`, `iam.roles.update`, `resourcemanager.projects.get`, `resourcemanager.projects.getIamPolicy`

### Role Viewer (`roles/iam.roleViewer`)

- Provides read access to all custom roles in the project. Lowest-level resources where you can grant this role: - Project
- Permissions (6): `iam.roles.get`, `iam.roles.list`, `iam.roles.listEffectiveTags`, `iam.roles.listTagBindings`, `resourcemanager.projects.get`, `resourcemanager.projects.getIamPolicy`

### IAM Workload Identity Pool Viewer (`roles/iam.workloadIdentityPoolViewer`)

- Launch stage: Beta
- Read access to workload identity pools.
- Permissions (15): `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolManagedIdentities.get`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolManagedIdentities.getAttestationRules`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolManagedIdentities.list`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolNamespaces.get`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolNamespaces.list`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolProviderKeys.get`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolProviderKeys.list`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolProviders.get`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPoolProviders.list`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPools.get`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPools.getAttestationRules`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPools.getIamPolicy`, `iam.googleapis.com/workloadIdentityPools.list`, `resourcemanager.projects.get`, `resourcemanager.projects.list`

### Workspace Pool IAM Admin (`roles/iam.workspacePoolAdmin`)

- IAM workspace pool admin able to bind IAM policies to Dasher accounts.
- Permissions (5): `iam.workspacePools.*`, `iam.googleapis.com/workspacePools.createPolicyBinding`, `iam.googleapis.com/workspacePools.deletePolicyBinding`, `iam.googleapis.com/workspacePools.searchPolicyBindings`, `iam.googleapis.com/workspacePools.updatePolicyBinding`
